import type { ConfigContext, ExpoConfig } from '@expo/config';

type ExpoPlugins = NonNullable<ExpoConfig['plugins']>;

export default ({ config }: ConfigContext): ExpoConfig => {
  const nativePlugins: ExpoPlugins =
    process.env.EXPO_PLATFORM === 'native'
      ? [['expo-dev-client', { launchMode: 'most-recent' }]]
      : [];

  return {
    ...config,
    name: 'DartMate',
    slug: 'dartmate',
    version: process.env.BILT_APP_VERSION ?? '1.0.0',
    orientation: 'portrait',
    userInterfaceStyle: 'automatic',
    scheme: 'dartmate',
    runtimeVersion: {
      policy: 'appVersion',
    },
    assetBundlePatterns: ['**/*'],
    ios: {
      deploymentTarget: '16.4',
      infoPlist: {
        ITSAppUsesNonExemptEncryption: false,
        NSCameraUsageDescription: 'Kamera slúži na voliteľný videohovor počas šípkového zápasu.',
        NSMicrophoneUsageDescription: 'Mikrofón slúži na rozhovor so súperom počas zápasu.',
      },
      supportsTablet: true,
      bundleIdentifier: process.env.BILT_IOS_BUNDLE_ID ?? 'me.bilt.dartmate',
    },
    android: {
      package: process.env.BILT_ANDROID_PACKAGE ?? 'me.bilt.dartmate',
      permissions: ['android.permission.CAMERA', 'android.permission.RECORD_AUDIO'],
    },
    web: {
      bundler: 'metro',
      // 'single' = SPA export: one index.html + client routing, so edge serving
      // needs only a single 404→index.html fallback rule.
      output: 'single',
      favicon: './public/icons/icon-192.png',
    },
    extra: {
      appStoreAppId: process.env.BILT_APP_STORE_APP_ID,
    },
    plugins: [
      'expo-router',
      'expo-font',
      'expo-splash-screen',
      '@livekit/react-native-expo-plugin',
      [
        '@config-plugins/react-native-webrtc',
        {
          cameraPermission: 'Kamera slúži na voliteľný videohovor počas šípkového zápasu.',
          microphonePermission: 'Mikrofón slúži na rozhovor so súperom počas zápasu.',
        },
      ],
      ...nativePlugins,
    ],
    experiments: {
      typedRoutes: true,
      reactCompiler: true,
    },
  };
};

================================================================================
FILE: app/_layout.tsx
================================================================================
// oxlint-disable-next-line eslint-plugin-import/no-unassigned-import
import '../global.css';

import { GestureHandlerRootView } from 'react-native-gesture-handler';
import {
  Inter_400Regular,
  Inter_500Medium,
  Inter_600SemiBold,
  Inter_700Bold,
  useFonts,
} from '@expo-google-fonts/inter';
import Constants, { ExecutionEnvironment } from 'expo-constants';
import { Platform } from 'react-native';
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import * as DevClient from 'expo-dev-client';
import { HeroUINativeProvider } from 'heroui-native';
import { Uniwind } from 'uniwind';
import {
  ErrorBoundary as ExpoErrorBoundary,
  type ErrorBoundaryProps,
  SplashScreen,
  Stack,
} from 'expo-router';

import { patchExpoGoAssetUrls } from '@/lib/expoGoAssets';
import { registerColorTokens } from '@/lib/registerColorTokens';
import { initPostHog } from '@/lib/posthog';
import { registerServiceWorker } from '@/lib/registerServiceWorker';
import { reportErrorToParent } from '@/lib/reportPreviewError';
import { InstallPrompt } from '@/components/InstallPrompt';
import { palette } from '@/lib/darts';
import { usePlayerSession } from '@/lib/players';
import { useOnlinePresence } from '@/lib/online';

/**
 * Custom ErrorBoundary that reports React render errors to the parent window (Bilt preview iframe)
 * and then renders the default Expo error UI.
 */
function ErrorBoundary({ error, retry }: ErrorBoundaryProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web' && error) {
      const message = [error.message, error.stack].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
      reportErrorToParent(message);
    }
  }, [error]);
  return <ExpoErrorBoundary error={error} retry={retry} />;
}

export { ErrorBoundary };

// Before any screen renders an Image or loads a font.
patchExpoGoAssetUrls();
registerColorTokens();

// Starter is light-only by default. Remove this when implementing requested dark mode.
Uniwind.setTheme('light');

void SplashScreen.preventAutoHideAsync();

export default function RootLayout() {
  usePlayerSession();
  useOnlinePresence();
  const [loaded, error] = useFonts({
    Inter_400Regular,
    Inter_500Medium,
    Inter_600SemiBold,
    Inter_700Bold,
  });

  // Report uncaught JS errors and unhandled promise rejections to parent (Bilt preview iframe)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS !== 'web' || typeof window === 'undefined') return undefined;

    const handleError = (event: ErrorEvent) => {
      const message = event.error?.stack ?? event.message ?? 'Unknown error';
      reportErrorToParent(message);
    };

    const handleUnhandledRejection = (event: PromiseRejectionEvent) => {
      const err = event.reason;
      const message =
        err instanceof Error ? [err.message, err.stack].filter(Boolean).join('\n') : String(err);
      reportErrorToParent(message);
    };

    window.addEventListener('error', handleError);
    window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection', handleUnhandledRejection);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('error', handleError);
      window.removeEventListener('unhandledrejection', handleUnhandledRejection);
    };
  }, []);

  // Inject Google Fonts link tag for web to ensure fonts load through proxy
  // Also register font family names as fallback if expo-font fails
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      // Check if link already exists
      const existingLink = document.querySelector(
        'link[href*="fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter"]',
      );

      if (!existingLink) {
        const link = document.createElement('link');
        link.rel = 'stylesheet';
        link.href =
          'https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap';
        link.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
        document.head.appendChild(link);
      }

      // Note: The @import in global.css and the link tag above ensure Inter font loads
      // expo-font will register the font family names (Inter_400Regular, etc.)
      // If expo-font fails due to proxy issues, the fonts should still be available
      // via the direct Google Fonts CDN link, though the specific font family names
      // might not be registered. The app should still render with Inter font.
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const isExpoGo = Constants.executionEnvironment === ExecutionEnvironment.StoreClient;
    if (__DEV__ && Platform.OS !== 'web' && !isExpoGo) {
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        DevClient.closeMenu();
        DevClient.hideMenu();
      }, 1000);
      return () => clearTimeout(timer);
    }
    return undefined;
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') {
      initPostHog();
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    registerServiceWorker();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (loaded || error) {
      void SplashScreen.hideAsync();
    }
  }, [loaded, error]);

  if (!loaded && !error) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
      <HeroUINativeProvider>
        <Stack
          screenOptions={{
            headerStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.paper },
            headerTintColor: palette.ink,
            headerTitleStyle: { fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' },
            headerShadowVisible: false,
            headerBackButtonDisplayMode: 'minimal',
            contentStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.paper },
          }}
        >
          <Stack.Screen name="(tabs)" options={{ headerShown: false }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="new-match" options={{ title: 'Nový zápas' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="match" options={{ title: 'Zápas' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="friend/new" options={{ title: 'Pridať priateľa' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="friend/[id]" options={{ title: 'Priateľ' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="account" options={{ title: 'Môj účet' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="online/index" options={{ title: 'Online zápasy' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="online/new" options={{ title: 'Pozvať na zápas' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="online/[id]" options={{ title: 'Online zápas' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="conversation/[id]" options={{ title: 'Správy' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="statistics" options={{ title: 'Štatistiky' }} />
          <Stack.Screen name="chat/[id]" options={{ title: 'Konverzácia' }} />
        </Stack>
        <InstallPrompt />
      </HeroUINativeProvider>
    </GestureHandlerRootView>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/(tabs)/_layout.tsx
================================================================================
import { Target, Users, MessageCircle } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { Tabs } from 'expo-router';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import { palette, feedback } from '@/lib/darts';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

export default function TabLayout() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const bottom = Math.max(insets.bottom, 8);
  return (
    <>
      {/* eslint-disable-next-line react/style-prop-object -- Expo StatusBar accepts a string appearance, not a React Native style object. */}
      <StatusBar style="dark" />
      <Tabs
        screenOptions={{
          headerShown: false,
          sceneStyle: { backgroundColor: palette.paper },
          tabBarStyle: {
            backgroundColor: palette.white,
            borderTopColor: palette.line,
            height: 68 + bottom,
            paddingTop: 8,
            paddingBottom: bottom,
            elevation: 0,
            shadowOpacity: 0,
          },
          tabBarActiveTintColor: palette.ink,
          tabBarInactiveTintColor: palette.muted,
          tabBarLabelStyle: { fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold', fontSize: 11, marginTop: 0 },
        }}
        screenListeners={({ navigation, route }) => ({
          tabPress: () => {
            if (navigation.getState().routes[navigation.getState().index]?.key !== route.key)
              feedback.selection();
          },
        })}
      >
        <Tabs.Screen
          name="index"
          options={{
            title: 'Hra',
            tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Target size={23} color={color} strokeWidth={1.8} />,
          }}
        />
        <Tabs.Screen
          name="friends"
          options={{
            title: 'Priatelia',
            tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <Users size={23} color={color} strokeWidth={1.8} />,
          }}
        />
        <Tabs.Screen
          name="messages"
          options={{
            title: 'Správy',
            tabBarIcon: ({ color }) => <MessageCircle size={23} color={color} strokeWidth={1.8} />,
          }}
        />
      </Tabs>
    </>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/(tabs)/friends.tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Button, Input, PressableFeedback } from 'heroui-native';
import { UserPlus, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { router, useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
import Animated, { FadeIn, FadeOut } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import {
  Heading,
  Txt,
  FriendRow,
  Avatar,
  Empty,
  LoadingRows,
  StorageNotice,
} from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { palette, useDarts } from '@/lib/darts';
import { usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';
import { loadContacts, useOnlineQuery } from '@/lib/online';
import { OnlineError, Presence } from '@/components/OnlineUI';

export default function Friends() {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('');
  const localFriends = useDarts((s) => s.friends);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const profile = usePlayers((s) => s.profile);
  const onlineFriends = usePlayers((s) => s.friends);
  const ready = usePlayers((s) => s.ready);
  const loading = usePlayers((s) => s.loading);
  const error = usePlayers((s) => s.error);
  const refresh = usePlayers((s) => s.refresh);
  const social = useOnlineQuery(
    session ? `friends:${session.user.id}` : null,
    useCallback(() => loadContacts(), []),
  );
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      void refresh();
    }, [refresh]),
  );
  const friends = session ? onlineFriends : localFriends;
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const busy = !ready || (!session && !hydrated) || loading;
  const shown = friends.filter((f) =>
    `${f.name} ${f.handle}`.toLocaleLowerCase('sk').includes(query.trim().toLocaleLowerCase('sk')),
  );
  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      className="bg-background flex-1"
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      style={{ paddingTop: insets.top }}
    >
      <FlatList
        data={busy || error ? [] : shown}
        keyExtractor={(f) => f.id}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          paddingBottom: 32,
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
          flexGrow: 1,
        }}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <View className="mb-6 flex-row items-center justify-between">
              <Heading>Priatelia</Heading>
              <Button
                variant="secondary"
                isIconOnly
                accessibilityLabel="Pridať priateľa"
                onPress={() => router.push('/friend/new')}
              >
                <UserPlus size={21} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              </Button>
            </View>
            <StorageNotice />
            <View className="mb-6 gap-3">
              {profile ? (
                <Button variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/account')}>
                  <Button.Label numberOfLines={1}>Môj nick: @{profile.nickname}</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Txt>
                    {session
                      ? 'Nastav si nick, aby ťa priatelia našli.'
                      : 'Nájdi priateľov, ktorí majú túto aplikáciu.'}
                  </Txt>
                  <Button onPress={() => router.push('/account')}>
                    <Button.Label>
                      {session ? 'Vybrať nick' : 'Prihlásiť sa a vybrať nick'}
                    </Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                </>
              )}
            </View>
            <OnlineError error={social.error} retry={() => void social.refresh()} />
            {session && (
              <Button className="mb-4" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/online')}>
                <Button.Label>Pozvánky a zápasy</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            )}
            <Input
              value={query}
              onChangeText={setQuery}
              placeholder="Hľadať v mojich priateľoch"
              accessibilityLabel="Hľadať v mojich priateľoch"
              className="rounded-xl"
            />
            <View className="mt-7 mb-1 flex-row justify-between gap-2">
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px]">
                {session ? 'Tvoja partia' : 'Ukážková partia'}
              </Txt>
              {!busy && (
                <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px]">
                  {shown.length}{' '}
                  {shown.length === 1
                    ? 'priateľ'
                    : shown.length < 5 && shown.length > 1
                      ? 'priatelia'
                      : 'priateľov'}
                </Txt>
              )}
            </View>
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <Animated.View entering={FadeIn.duration(180)} exiting={FadeOut.duration(120)}>
            {session ? (
              <PressableFeedback
                onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/friend/[id]', params: { id: item.id } })}
                className="border-border flex-row items-center gap-3 border-b py-4"
              >
                <Avatar friend={item} />
                <View className="flex-1">
                  <Txt numberOfLines={1} style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>
                    @{item.handle}
                  </Txt>
                  <View className="mt-2">
                    <Presence
                      online={!!social.data?.find((c) => c.user_id === item.id)?.online}
                      unknown={social.loading || !!social.error}
                    />
                  </View>
                </View>
                <ChevronRight size={20} color={palette.muted} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              </PressableFeedback>
            ) : (
              <FriendRow
                friend={item}
                detail={
                  item.demo ? `@${item.handle} · ukážkový hráč` : `@${item.handle} · lokálny hráč`
                }
              />
            )}
          </Animated.View>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          busy ? (
            <LoadingRows />
          ) : error ? (
            <View className="gap-4 py-6">
              <Txt className="text-danger" accessibilityRole="alert">
                {error}
              </Txt>
              <Button variant="outline" onPress={() => void refresh()}>
                <Button.Label>Skúsiť znova</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <Empty
              text={
                query
                  ? 'Takého priateľa tu zatiaľ nemáš.'
                  : 'Tu budú hráči, ktorých pridáš podľa nicku.'
              }
              action={query ? 'Vymazať hľadanie' : 'Pridať priateľa'}
              onPress={() => (query ? setQuery('') : router.push('/friend/new'))}
            />
          )
        }
        ListFooterComponent={
          <Button variant="outline" onPress={() => router.push('/friend/new')} className="mt-6">
            <UserPlus size={19} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            <Button.Label>Pridať podľa nicku</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        }
      />
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

import { View, useWindowDimensions } from 'react-native';
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, PressableFeedback } from 'heroui-native';
import { ArrowUpRight, Target, Video, ChevronRight, Trophy } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { Page, Txt, Heading, FriendRow, StorageNotice, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { Dartboard } from '@/components/Dartboard';
import { palette, useDarts } from '@/lib/darts';
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';
import { loadContacts, loadOpenMatches, useOnlineQuery } from '@/lib/online';
import { OpenMatches, OnlineError, Presence } from '@/components/OnlineUI';

export default function Home() {
  const compact = useWindowDimensions().width < 360;
  const friends = useDarts((s) => s.friends);
  const game = useDarts((s) => s.game);
  const history = useDarts((s) => s.history);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const social = useOnlineQuery(
    session ? `home:${session.user.id}` : null,
    useCallback(async () => {
      const [contacts, matches] = await Promise.all([loadContacts(), loadOpenMatches()]);
      return { contacts, matches };
    }, []),
  );
  return (
    <Page safeTop>
      <View className="mb-7 flex-row items-center justify-between">
        <View className="flex-row items-center gap-2">
          <Target size={28} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          <Txt className="text-[28px]" style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold', letterSpacing: -1 }}>
            oche
          </Txt>
        </View>
        <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onPress={() => router.push('/account')}>
          <Button.Label>Môj účet</Button.Label>
        </Button>
      </View>
      <StorageNotice />
      <Heading className="mb-5">Ideš na šípky?</Heading>
      <View className="bg-ink overflow-hidden rounded-[24px] p-5">
        <View className="flex-row items-start justify-between gap-2">
          <View className="flex-1">
            <Txt
              className="text-[28px] leading-[34px] text-white"
              style={{
                fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold',
                letterSpacing: -0.8,
                fontSize: compact ? 24 : 28,
                lineHeight: compact ? 30 : 34,
              }}
            >
              Terč doma.{'\n'}Súper kdekoľvek.
            </Txt>
          </View>
          <ArrowUpRight size={25} strokeWidth={1.8} color={palette.yellow} />
        </View>
        <View className="my-4 flex-row items-center justify-between">
          <View style={{ flexShrink: 0, width: compact ? 112 : 144 }}>
            <Txt
              numberOfLines={1}
              className="text-accent"
              style={{
                fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold',
                letterSpacing: -4,
                fontSize: compact ? 56 : 68,
                lineHeight: compact ? 66 : 76,
              }}
            >
              501
            </Txt>
          </View>
          <View style={{ marginRight: -10 }}>
            <Dartboard size={compact ? 120 : 154} />
          </View>
        </View>
        <Button size="lg" onPress={() => router.push('/online')} className="rounded-xl">
          <Button.Label style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>Hrať online</Button.Label>
          <ArrowUpRight size={20} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        </Button>
      </View>
      <View className="mt-4 mb-7 flex-row items-center gap-2">
        <Video size={16} color={palette.muted} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <Txt className="text-muted flex-1 text-[12px]">
          Kamera je voliteľná · skóre zadávaš ručne
        </Txt>
      </View>
      <Button variant="secondary" className="mb-4" onPress={() => router.push('/statistics')}>
        <Trophy size={18} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <Button.Label>Moje štatistiky</Button.Label>
      </Button>
      <OnlineError error={social.error} retry={() => void social.refresh()} />
      {social.data && session && (
        <OpenMatches matches={social.data.matches} userId={session.user.id} />
      )}
      <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between">
        <Heading className="text-[20px]">Tvoja partia</Heading>
        <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onPress={() => router.push('/friends')}>
          <Button.Label>Všetci</Button.Label>
          <ChevronRight size={16} color={palette.ink} />
        </Button>
      </View>
      {session ? (
        social.loading ? (
          <LoadingRows />
        ) : social.data?.contacts.length ? (
          social.data.contacts.slice(0, 3).map((contact) => (
            <PressableFeedback
              key={contact.user_id}
              className="border-border gap-2 border-b py-4"
              onPress={() =>
                router.push({
                  pathname: '/conversation/[id]',
                  params: { id: contact.user_id ?? '' },
                })
              }
            >
              <Txt numberOfLines={1} style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>
                @{contact.nickname}
              </Txt>
              <Presence online={!!contact.online} unknown={!!social.error} />
            </PressableFeedback>
          ))
        ) : (
          <Button variant="outline" className="mt-4" onPress={() => router.push('/friend/new')}>
            <Button.Label>Pridať priateľa podľa nicku</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        )
      ) : (
        <>
          <Txt className="text-muted mt-1 text-[12px]">
            Ukážkoví hráči na vyskúšanie lokálneho zápasu
          </Txt>
          {hydrated ? (
            friends
              .slice(0, 3)
              .map((friend) => <FriendRow key={friend.id} friend={friend} action="play" />)
          ) : (
            <LoadingRows />
          )}
        </>
      )}
      {game && (
        <Button className="mt-5" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/match')}>
          <Button.Label>Pokračovať v lokálnom zápase</Button.Label>
        </Button>
      )}
      {history.length > 0 && (
        <View className="mt-7">
          <Heading className="mb-3 text-[20px]">Lokálne výsledky</Heading>
          {history.slice(0, 3).map((g) => (
            <View key={g.id} className="border-border flex-row items-center gap-3 border-b py-3">
              <Trophy size={22} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
              <View className="flex-1">
                <Txt style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {g.winner === 0 ? 'Výhra' : 'Prehra'} proti {g.opponentName}
                </Txt>
                <Txt className="text-muted mt-1 text-[12px]">
                  {g.starting} · {g.doubleOut ? 'Double out' : 'Straight out'} ·{' '}
                  {new Date(g.date).toLocaleDateString('sk-SK')}
                </Txt>
              </View>
            </View>
          ))}
        </View>
      )}
      <PressableFeedback
        className="mt-6 flex-row items-center gap-3 py-3"
        onPress={() => router.push('/new-match')}
      >
        <Target size={20} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
        <Txt className="flex-1" style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_500Medium' }}>
          Tréning pri jednom terči
        </Txt>
        <ChevronRight size={18} color={palette.muted} />
      </PressableFeedback>
    </Page>
  );
}

FILE: app/(tabs)/messages.tsx

import { FlatList, View } from 'react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Button, PressableFeedback } from 'heroui-native';
import { router } from 'expo-router';
import { Heading, Txt, Avatar, Empty, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { useDarts } from '@/lib/darts';
import { useCallback } from 'react';
import { usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';
import { loadContacts, loadOpenMatches, timeLabel, useOnlineQuery } from '@/lib/online';
import { OnlineGate, OnlineError, Presence, OpenMatches } from '@/components/OnlineUI';

export default function Messages() {
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const ready = usePlayers((s) => s.ready);
  if (!ready)
    return (
      <View className="bg-background flex-1 p-5">
        <LoadingRows />
      </View>
    );
  return session ? <OnlineMessages userId={session.user.id} /> : <LocalMessages />;
}
function OnlineMessages({ userId }: { userId: string }) {
  const query = useOnlineQuery(
    `inbox:${userId}`,
    useCallback(async () => {
      const [contacts, matches] = await Promise.all([loadContacts(), loadOpenMatches()]);
      return { contacts, matches };
    }, []),
  );
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  return (
    <View className="bg-background flex-1" style={{ paddingTop: insets.top }}>
      <FlatList
        data={query.data?.contacts.filter((c) => c.last_body) ?? []}
        keyExtractor={(c) => c.user_id ?? ''}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          paddingBottom: 32,
          flexGrow: 1,
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
        }}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View className="mb-5">
            <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <Heading>Správy</Heading>
              <Button variant="secondary" size="sm" onPress={() => router.push('/friends')}>
                <Button.Label>Napísať</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            </View>
            <OnlineError error={query.error} retry={() => void query.refresh()} />
            {query.data && <OpenMatches matches={query.data.matches} userId={userId} />}
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <PressableFeedback
            className="border-border flex-row items-start gap-3 border-b py-5"
            onPress={() =>
              router.push({ pathname: '/conversation/[id]', params: { id: item.user_id ?? '' } })
            }
          >
            <View className="flex-1 gap-2">
              <View className="flex-row gap-3">
                <Txt
                  className="flex-1"
                  numberOfLines={1}
                  style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                >
                  @{item.nickname}
                </Txt>
                {item.last_message_at && (
                  <Txt className="text-muted text-[11px]">{timeLabel(item.last_message_at)}</Txt>
                )}
              </View>
              <Presence online={!!item.online} unknown={!!query.error} />
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px] leading-5" numberOfLines={2}>
                {item.last_sender_id === userId ? 'Ty: ' : ''}
                {item.last_body}
              </Txt>
            </View>
          </PressableFeedback>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <OnlineGate>
            {query.loading ? (
              <LoadingRows />
            ) : !query.error ? (
              <Empty
                chat
                text="Tu nájdeš správy od priateľov."
                action="Napísať priateľovi"
                onPress={() => router.push('/friends')}
              />
            ) : null}
          </OnlineGate>
        }
      />
    </View>
  );
}

function LocalMessages() {
  const friends = useDarts((s) => s.friends);
  const messages = useDarts((s) => s.messages);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const conversations = friends
    .map((friend) => ({ friend, last: messages.filter((m) => m.friendId === friend.id).at(-1) }))
    .filter((c) => c.last);
  return (
    <View className="bg-background flex-1" style={{ paddingTop: insets.top }}>
      <FlatList
        data={hydrated ? conversations : []}
        keyExtractor={(c) => c.friend.id}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          flexGrow: 1,
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
        }}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View className="mb-5">
            <Heading>Správy</Heading>
            <Button variant="secondary" className="mt-4" onPress={() => router.push('/account')}>
              <Button.Label>Prihlásiť sa pre skutočné správy</Button.Label>
            </Button>
            <Txt className="text-muted mt-3 text-[13px]">
              Ukážkové konverzácie · správy ostávajú u teba
            </Txt>
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <PressableFeedback
            onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/chat/[id]', params: { id: item.friend.id } })}
            className="border-border flex-row gap-3 border-b py-5"
          >
            <Avatar friend={item.friend} size={52} />
            <View className="flex-1 gap-2">
              <View className="flex-row gap-2">
                <Txt
                  className="flex-1"
                  numberOfLines={1}
                  style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                >
                  {item.friend.name}
                </Txt>
                <Txt className="text-muted text-[11px]">{item.last?.time}</Txt>
              </View>
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px] leading-5" numberOfLines={2}>
                {item.last?.mine ? 'Ty: ' : ''}
                {item.last?.text}
              </Txt>
            </View>
          </PressableFeedback>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          !hydrated ? (
            <LoadingRows />
          ) : (
            <Empty
              chat
              text="Tu nájdeš svoje konverzácie."
              action="Vybrať priateľa"
              onPress={() => router.push('/friends')}
            />
          )
        }
      />
    </View>
  );
}
