================================================================================
FILE: app/account.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, Input, Label, TextField } from 'heroui-native';
import { Page, Heading, Txt, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { bilt } from '@/lib/bilt';
import { feedback } from '@/lib/darts';
import { playerError, usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';

export default function Account() {
  const ready = usePlayers((s) => s.ready);
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const profile = usePlayers((s) => s.profile);
  const loading = usePlayers((s) => s.loading);
  const remoteError = usePlayers((s) => s.error);
  const refresh = usePlayers((s) => s.refresh);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [sentEmail, setSentEmail] = useState('');
  const [code, setCode] = useState('');
  const [nick, setNick] = useState('');
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const fail = (message: string) => {
    setError(message);
    feedback.error();
  };
  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (e) {
      fail(playerError({ message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Pripojenie zlyhalo.' }));
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  const send = () =>
    run(async () => {
      const clean = (sentEmail || email).trim().toLowerCase();
      if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(clean)) {
        fail('Zadaj platný e-mail.');
        return;
      }
      const { error: e } = await bilt.auth.signInWithOtp({ email: clean });
      if (e) {
        fail(playerError(e));
        return;
      }
      setSentEmail(clean);
      setCode('');
      feedback.success();
    });
  const verify = () =>
    run(async () => {
      if (!/^\d{6}$/.test(code)) {
        fail('Zadaj 6-miestny kód z e-mailu.');
        return;
      }
      const { error: e } = await bilt.auth.verifyOtp({
        email: sentEmail,
        token: code,
        type: 'email',
      });
      if (e) {
        fail(playerError(e));
        return;
      }
      feedback.success();
    });
  const save = () =>
    run(async () => {
      if (!session) return;
      const nickname = nick.trim().replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase();
      if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/.test(nickname)) {
        fail('Nick musí mať 3 až 24 znakov: písmená bez diakritiky, čísla alebo podčiarkovník.');
        return;
      }
      const { error: e } = await bilt
        .from('player_profiles')
        .insert({ user_id: session.user.id, nickname });
      if (e) {
        fail(playerError(e));
        return;
      }
      await refresh();
      if (usePlayers.getState().error) return;
      feedback.success();
      router.dismissTo('/friends');
    });
  return (
    <Page form fallbackHref="/friends" fallbackLabel="Späť k priateľom">
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Môj účet' }} />
      {!ready || (session && loading && !profile) ? (
        <LoadingRows />
      ) : (
        <View className="mt-3 gap-5">
          <Heading>
            {profile ? `@${profile.nickname}` : session ? 'Vyber si nick' : 'Prihlás sa'}
          </Heading>
          {!session ? (
            sentEmail ? (
              <>
                <Txt className="text-muted">Kód sme poslali na {sentEmail}.</Txt>
                <TextField isRequired>
                  <Label>Kód z e-mailu</Label>
                  <Input
                    value={code}
                    onChangeText={(v) => setCode(v.replace(/\D/g, ''))}
                    keyboardType="number-pad"
                    autoComplete="one-time-code"
                    maxLength={6}
                    placeholder="6-miestny kód"
                    editable={!pending}
                  />
                </TextField>
                <Button isDisabled={pending} onPress={verify}>
                  <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Overujem…' : 'Overiť kód'}</Button.Label>
                </Button>
                <Button variant="outline" isDisabled={pending} onPress={send}>
                  <Button.Label>Poslať nový kód</Button.Label>
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  isDisabled={pending}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setSentEmail('');
                    setCode('');
                    setError('');
                  }}
                >
                  <Button.Label>Zmeniť e-mail</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Txt className="text-muted">Prihlásenie aj vytvorenie účtu cez kód z e-mailu.</Txt>
                <TextField isRequired>
                  <Label>E-mail</Label>
                  <Input
                    value={email}
                    onChangeText={setEmail}
                    keyboardType="email-address"
                    autoCapitalize="none"
                    autoCorrect={false}
                    autoComplete="email"
                    placeholder="tvoj@email.sk"
                    editable={!pending}
                  />
                </TextField>
                <Button isDisabled={pending} onPress={send}>
                  <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Posielam…' : 'Poslať kód'}</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </>
            )
          ) : profile ? (
            <>
              <Txt className="text-muted">Tento nick daj priateľom, aby ťa našli v aplikácii.</Txt>
              <Button onPress={() => router.push('/friend/new')}>
                <Button.Label>Pridať priateľa podľa nicku</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : !remoteError ? (
            <>
              <TextField isRequired>
                <Label>Tvoj nick</Label>
                <Input
                  value={nick}
                  onChangeText={setNick}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  maxLength={24}
                  placeholder="napr. martin_180"
                  editable={!pending}
                />
              </TextField>
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px]">
                3 až 24 znakov: písmená bez diakritiky, čísla alebo podčiarkovník. Nick bude
                viditeľný ostatným hráčom.
              </Txt>
              <Button isDisabled={pending} onPress={save}>
                <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Ukladám…' : 'Uložiť nick'}</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            </>
          ) : null}
          {!!(error || remoteError) && (
            <Txt accessibilityRole="alert" className="text-danger">
              {error || remoteError}
            </Txt>
          )}
          {!!remoteError && (
            <Button variant="outline" onPress={() => void refresh()} isDisabled={loading}>
              <Button.Label>Skúsiť znova</Button.Label>
            </Button>
          )}
          {!!session && (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              isDisabled={pending}
              onPress={() =>
                run(async () => {
                  const { error: e } = await bilt.auth.signOut();
                  if (e) {
                    fail(playerError(e));
                    return;
                  }
                  setSentEmail('');
                  setCode('');
                  feedback.success();
                  router.dismissTo('/friends');
                })
              }
            >
              <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Čakaj…' : 'Odhlásiť sa'}</Button.Label>
            </Button>
          )}
        </View>
      )}
    </Page>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/chat/[id].tsx
================================================================================
import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, Input } from 'heroui-native';
import { Send, Target } from 'lucide-react-native';
import Animated, { FadeIn, FadeOut } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Txt, Empty, StorageNotice, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { palette, feedback, useDarts, type Message } from '@/lib/darts';

export default function Chat() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const friends = useDarts((s) => s.friends);
  const allMessages = useDarts((s) => s.messages);
  const sendMessage = useDarts((s) => s.sendMessage);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const friend = friends.find((f) => f.id === id);
  const messages = allMessages.filter((m) => m.friendId === id);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const list = useRef<FlatList<Message>>(null);
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const send = () => {
    if (!text.trim() || !friend) return;
    sendMessage(id, text.trim());
    setText('');
    feedback.success();
  };
  if (!hydrated)
    return (
      <View className="bg-background flex-1 p-5">
        <LoadingRows />
      </View>
    );
  if (!friend)
    return (
      <View className="bg-background flex-1 p-5">
        <Empty
          text="Tohto priateľa tu nemáš."
          action="Späť k priateľom"
          onPress={() => router.replace('/friends')}
        />
      </View>
    );
  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      className="bg-background flex-1"
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={90}
    >
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: friend.name }} />
      <View className="w-full max-w-xl flex-1 self-center">
        <View className="gap-3 px-5 pt-4">
          <StorageNotice />
          <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
            <Txt className="text-muted flex-1 text-[12px]">Prototyp · správy sa neodosielajú</Txt>
            <Button
              variant="secondary"
              size="sm"
              onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/new-match', params: { friendId: id } })}
            >
              <Target size={17} color={palette.ink} />
              <Button.Label>Hrať</Button.Label>
            </Button>
          </View>
        </View>
        <FlatList
          ref={list}
          data={messages}
          keyExtractor={(m) => m.id}
          contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12, flexGrow: 1 }}
          onContentSizeChange={() => list.current?.scrollToEnd({ animated: true })}
          renderItem={({ item }) => (
            <Animated.View
              entering={FadeIn.duration(160)}
              exiting={FadeOut.duration(100)}
              style={{ alignSelf: item.mine ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start', maxWidth: '85%' }}
            >
              <View
                className={`rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 ${item.mine ? 'bg-ink rounded-br-sm' : 'bg-surface rounded-bl-sm'}`}
              >
                <Txt className={item.mine ? 'leading-6 text-white' : 'leading-6'}>{item.text}</Txt>
                <Txt
                  className={`mt-2 text-right text-[11px] ${item.mine ? 'text-on-ink' : 'text-muted'}`}
                >
                  {item.time}
                </Txt>
              </View>
            </Animated.View>
          )}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <Empty
              chat
              text="Tu sa začne vaša konverzácia."
              action="Napísať pozdrav"
              onPress={() => setText('Ahoj, dáme si šípky?')}
            />
          }
        />
        <View
          className="bg-surface border-border border-t px-4 pt-3"
          style={{ paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 16) }}
        >
          <View className="flex-row items-end gap-2">
            <Input
              className="bg-background max-h-28 flex-1"
              placeholder="Napíš správu…"
              accessibilityLabel="Správa"
              multiline
              value={text}
              onChangeText={setText}
              maxLength={2000}
            />
            <Button
              isIconOnly
              isDisabled={!text.trim()}
              accessibilityLabel="Uložiť správu"
              onPress={send}
            >
              <Send size={21} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            </Button>
          </View>
          <Button
            className="mt-1"
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            onPress={() => router.dismissTo('/messages')}
          >
            <Button.Label>Všetky správy</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        </View>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/conversation/[id].tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Button, Input } from 'heroui-native';
import { Send } from 'lucide-react-native';
import * as Crypto from 'expo-crypto';
import Animated, { FadeIn, FadeOut } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { Txt, Empty, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { OnlineGate, OnlineError, Presence, OpenMatches } from '@/components/OnlineUI';
import { feedback, palette } from '@/lib/darts';
import { bilt } from '@/lib/bilt';
import { playerError, usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';
import {
  loadContacts,
  loadOpenMatches,
  timeLabel,
  useOnlineQuery,
  uuidPattern,
  type DirectMessage,
} from '@/lib/online';

export default function Conversation() {
  const { id = '' } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const userId = usePlayers((s) => s.session?.user.id);
  return <ConversationContent key={`${userId ?? 'guest'}:${id}`} id={id} userId={userId} />;
}
function ConversationContent({ id, userId }: { id: string; userId: string | undefined }) {
  const key = userId && uuidPattern.test(id) ? `${userId}:${id}` : null;
  const loader = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!userId || !uuidPattern.test(id)) return null;
    const [result, contacts, matches] = await Promise.all([
      bilt
        .from('direct_messages')
        .select('*')
        .or(
          `and(sender_id.eq.${userId},recipient_id.eq.${id}),and(sender_id.eq.${id},recipient_id.eq.${userId})`,
        )
        .order('id', { ascending: false })
        .limit(50),
      loadContacts(),
      loadOpenMatches(),
    ]);
    if (result.error) throw new Error(result.error.message);
    return {
      messages: result.data ?? [],
      contact: contacts.find((c) => c.user_id === id),
      matches: matches.filter((m) => m.host_id === id || m.guest_id === id),
    };
  }, [id, userId]);
  const query = useOnlineQuery(key, loader);
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [older, setOlder] = useState<{
    key: string;
    rows: DirectMessage[];
    hasMore: boolean;
  } | null>(null);
  const [previousPage, setPreviousPage] = useState(query.data);
  if (query.data !== previousPage) {
    setPreviousPage(query.data);
    if (key && query.data) {
      const previous = older?.key === key ? older : null;
      const merged = [
        ...new Map(
          [...(previous?.rows ?? []), ...query.data.messages].map((m) => [m.id, m]),
        ).values(),
      ];
      setOlder({
        key,
        rows: merged,
        hasMore: previous?.hasMore ?? query.data.messages.length === 50,
      });
    }
  }
  const [loadingOlder, setLoadingOlder] = useState(false);
  const [historyError, setHistoryError] = useState('');
  const request = useRef<{ key: string; body: string; id: string } | null>(null);
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const messages = useMemo(() => {
    const rows = [...(query.data?.messages ?? []), ...(older?.key === key ? older.rows : [])];
    return [...new Map(rows.map((m) => [m.id, m])).values()].sort((a, b) => b.id - a.id);
  }, [query.data, older, key]);
  const send = async () => {
    const body = text.trim();
    if (!body || pending || !key) return;
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    if (request.current?.body !== body || request.current.key !== key)
      request.current = { key, body, id: Crypto.randomUUID() };
    try {
      const { data, error: e } = await bilt.rpc('send_direct_message', {
        peer: id,
        message_body: body,
        client_request: request.current.id,
      });
      if (e) throw new Error(e.message);
      if (!data?.[0])
        throw new Error('Správu nemožno odoslať. Skontroluj, či ste stále priatelia.');
      if (usePlayers.getState().session?.user.id !== userId) return;
      // Keep sent messages when they leave the latest page.
      setOlder((s) => ({
        key,
        rows: [...(s?.key === key ? s.rows : []), data[0]],
        hasMore: s?.key === key ? s.hasMore : (query.data?.messages.length ?? 0) === 50,
      }));
      setText('');
      request.current = null;
      feedback.success();
      await query.refresh();
    } catch (e) {
      if (usePlayers.getState().session?.user.id === userId) {
        setError(
          playerError({
            message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Správu sa nepodarilo odoslať.',
          }),
        );
        feedback.error();
      }
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  const loadOlder = async () => {
    if (!userId || !key || loadingOlder || !messages.length) return;
    setLoadingOlder(true);
    setHistoryError('');
    try {
      const { data, error: e } = await bilt
        .from('direct_messages')
        .select('*')
        .or(
          `and(sender_id.eq.${userId},recipient_id.eq.${id}),and(sender_id.eq.${id},recipient_id.eq.${userId})`,
        )
        .lt('id', messages.at(-1)!.id)
        .order('id', { ascending: false })
        .limit(50);
      if (e) throw new Error(e.message);
      if (usePlayers.getState().session?.user.id !== userId) return;
      setOlder((s) => ({
        key,
        rows: [...(s?.key === key ? s.rows : []), ...(data ?? [])],
        hasMore: (data?.length ?? 0) === 50,
      }));
    } catch (e) {
      setHistoryError(
        playerError({
          message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Staršie správy sa nepodarilo načítať.',
        }),
      );
      feedback.error();
    } finally {
      setLoadingOlder(false);
    }
  };
  const contact = query.data?.contact;
  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      className="bg-background flex-1"
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={90}
    >
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: contact?.nickname ? `@${contact.nickname}` : 'Správy' }} />
      <View className="w-full max-w-xl flex-1 self-center">
        <View className="px-5 pt-3">
          <OnlineGate>
            {query.loading ? (
              <LoadingRows />
            ) : contact ? (
              <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <Presence online={!!contact.online} unknown={!!query.error} />
                <Button
                  size="sm"
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/online/new', params: { friendId: id } })}
                >
                  <Button.Label>Pozvať na zápas</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            ) : !query.error ? (
              <Empty
                text="Táto konverzácia nie je dostupná."
                action="Vybrať priateľa"
                onPress={() => router.replace('/friends')}
              />
            ) : null}
          </OnlineGate>
          <OnlineError error={query.error} retry={() => void query.refresh()} />
          {query.data && userId && <OpenMatches matches={query.data.matches} userId={userId} />}
        </View>
        <FlatList
          inverted
          data={messages}
          keyExtractor={(m) => String(m.id)}
          contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 20, gap: 12, flexGrow: 1 }}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          renderItem={({ item }) => {
            const mine = item.sender_id === userId;
            return (
              <Animated.View
                entering={FadeIn.duration(160)}
                exiting={FadeOut.duration(100)}
                style={{ alignSelf: mine ? 'flex-end' : 'flex-start', maxWidth: '85%' }}
              >
                <View
                  className={
                    mine
                      ? 'bg-ink rounded-2xl rounded-br-sm px-4 py-3'
                      : 'bg-surface rounded-2xl rounded-bl-sm px-4 py-3'
                  }
                >
                  <Txt className={mine ? 'leading-6 text-white' : 'leading-6'}>{item.body}</Txt>
                  <Txt
                    className={
                      mine
                        ? 'text-on-ink mt-2 text-right text-[11px]'
                        : 'text-muted mt-2 text-right text-[11px]'
                    }
                  >
                    {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleDateString('sk-SK')} ·{' '}
                    {timeLabel(item.created_at)}
                    {mine ? ' · Odoslané' : ''}
                  </Txt>
                </View>
              </Animated.View>
            );
          }}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            contact && !query.loading && !query.error ? (
              <Empty
                chat
                text="Napíš priateľovi, či si chce zahrať."
                action="Napísať pozdrav"
                onPress={() => setText('Ahoj, dáme si šípky?')}
              />
            ) : undefined
          }
          ListFooterComponent={
            <View>
              <OnlineError error={historyError} retry={() => void loadOlder()} />
              {(older?.key === key ? older.hasMore : (query.data?.messages.length ?? 0) === 50) &&
                messages.length > 0 && (
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    isDisabled={loadingOlder}
                    onPress={() => void loadOlder()}
                  >
                    <Button.Label>{loadingOlder ? 'Načítavam…' : 'Staršie správy'}</Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                )}
            </View>
          }
        />
        <View
          className="bg-surface border-border border-t px-4 pt-3"
          style={{ paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 16) }}
        >
          {!!error && (
            <Txt className="text-danger mb-2 text-[13px]" accessibilityRole="alert">
              {error}
            </Txt>
          )}
          {contact && (
            <View className="flex-row items-end gap-2">
              <Input
                className="bg-background max-h-28 flex-1"
                value={text}
                multiline
                maxLength={2000}
                editable={!pending}
                placeholder="Napíš správu…"
                accessibilityLabel="Správa"
                onChangeText={setText}
              />
              <Button
                isIconOnly
                isDisabled={pending || !text.trim() || !!query.error}
                accessibilityLabel={pending ? 'Odosielam správu' : 'Odoslať správu'}
                onPress={() => void send()}
              >
                <Send size={21} strokeWidth={1.8} color={palette.ink} />
              </Button>
            </View>
          )}
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            className="mt-1"
            onPress={() => router.dismissTo('/messages')}
          >
            <Button.Label>Všetky správy</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        </View>
      </View>
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/friend/[id].tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router, useFocusEffect, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Button } from 'heroui-native';
import { Page, Heading, Txt, Avatar, LoadingRows, Empty } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { usePlayers, playerError } from '@/lib/players';
import { bilt } from '@/lib/bilt';
import { feedback } from '@/lib/darts';
import { loadContacts, useOnlineQuery } from '@/lib/online';
import { OnlineError, Presence } from '@/components/OnlineUI';

export default function PlayerFriend() {
  const { id } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id: string }>();
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const ready = usePlayers((s) => s.ready);
  const friends = usePlayers((s) => s.friends);
  const loading = usePlayers((s) => s.loading);
  const remoteError = usePlayers((s) => s.error);
  const refresh = usePlayers((s) => s.refresh);
  const friend = friends.find((f) => f.id === id);
  const social = useOnlineQuery(
    session ? `friend:${session.user.id}:${id}` : null,
    useCallback(() => loadContacts(), []),
  );
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      void refresh();
    }, [refresh]),
  );
  const remove = async () => {
    if (!session || !friend) return;
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const { error: e } = await bilt
        .from('player_friends')
        .delete()
        .eq('owner_id', session.user.id)
        .eq('friend_id', friend.id);
      if (e) {
        setError(playerError(e));
        feedback.error();
        return;
      }
      await refresh();
      feedback.success();
      router.dismissTo('/friends');
    } catch (e) {
      setError(playerError({ message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Pripojenie zlyhalo.' }));
      feedback.error();
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  return (
    <Page fallbackHref="/friends" fallbackLabel="Späť k priateľom">
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Priateľ' }} />
      {!ready || (loading && !friend) ? (
        <LoadingRows />
      ) : remoteError ? (
        <View className="gap-4">
          <Txt className="text-danger">{remoteError}</Txt>
          <Button onPress={() => void refresh()}>
            <Button.Label>Skúsiť znova</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        </View>
      ) : !friend ? (
        <Empty
          text="Tohto hráča nemáš medzi priateľmi."
          action="Otvoriť priateľov"
          onPress={() => router.replace('/friends')}
        />
      ) : (
        <View className="mt-4 gap-5">
          <Avatar friend={friend} size={64} />
          <Heading>@{friend.handle}</Heading>
          <Presence
            online={!!social.data?.find((c) => c.user_id === id)?.online}
            unknown={social.loading || !!social.error}
          />
          <OnlineError error={social.error} retry={() => void social.refresh()} />
          <Button
            onPress={() =>
              router.push({ pathname: '/conversation/[id]', params: { id: friend.id } })
            }
          >
            <Button.Label>Napísať správu</Button.Label>
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="secondary"
            onPress={() =>
              router.push({ pathname: '/online/new', params: { friendId: friend.id } })
            }
          >
            <Button.Label>Pozvať na online zápas</Button.Label>
          </Button>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            onPress={() => router.push({ pathname: '/new-match', params: { friendId: friend.id } })}
          >
            <Button.Label>Lokálny zápas</Button.Label>
          </Button>
          {confirm ? (
            <View className="gap-3">
              <Txt>Odobrať @{friend.handle} z priateľov?</Txt>
              <Button variant="danger" isDisabled={pending} onPress={remove}>
                <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Odoberám…' : 'Odobrať priateľa'}</Button.Label>
              </Button>
              <Button variant="ghost" isDisabled={pending} onPress={() => setConfirm(false)}>
                <Button.Label>Zrušiť</Button.Label>
              </Button>
            </View>
          ) : (
            <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => setConfirm(true)}>
              <Button.Label>Odobrať priateľa</Button.Label>
            </Button>
          )}
          {!!error && (
            <Txt className="text-danger" accessibilityRole="alert">
              {error}
            </Txt>
          )}
        </View>
      )}
    </Page>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/friend/new.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, TextField, Input, Label } from 'heroui-native';
import type { Tables } from '@biltme/backend';
import { UserSearch } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { Page, Txt, Heading, LoadingRows, Empty } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { palette, feedback } from '@/lib/darts';
import { bilt } from '@/lib/bilt';
import { usePlayers, playerError } from '@/lib/players';

type FoundPlayer = Pick<Tables<'player_profiles'>, 'user_id' | 'nickname'>;
export default function NewFriend() {
  const session = usePlayers((s) => s.session);
  const profile = usePlayers((s) => s.profile);
  const ready = usePlayers((s) => s.ready);
  const loading = usePlayers((s) => s.loading);
  const friends = usePlayers((s) => s.friends);
  const remoteError = usePlayers((s) => s.error);
  const refresh = usePlayers((s) => s.refresh);
  const [nick, setNick] = useState('');
  const [found, setFound] = useState<FoundPlayer | null>(null);
  const [searched, setSearched] = useState(false);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const fail = (message: string) => {
    setError(message);
    feedback.error();
  };
  const run = async (action: () => Promise<void>) => {
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await action();
    } catch (e) {
      fail(playerError({ message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Pripojenie zlyhalo.' }));
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  const search = () =>
    run(async () => {
      setFound(null);
      setSearched(false);
      const clean = nick.trim().replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase();
      if (!/^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$/.test(clean)) {
        fail('Zadaj celý nick hráča (3 až 24 znakov).');
        return;
      }
      if (clean === profile?.nickname) {
        fail('Toto je tvoj vlastný nick.');
        return;
      }
      const { data, error: e } = await bilt.rpc('find_player_by_nickname', {
        search_nickname: clean,
      });
      if (e) {
        fail(playerError(e));
        return;
      }
      const player = data?.[0];
      if (player && (!player.user_id || !player.nickname)) {
        fail('Výsledok vyhľadávania sa nepodarilo načítať. Skús znova.');
        return;
      }
      setFound(
        player?.user_id && player.nickname
          ? { user_id: player.user_id, nickname: player.nickname }
          : null,
      );
      setSearched(true);
    });
  const add = () =>
    run(async () => {
      if (!found || !session) return;
      const { error: e } = await bilt
        .from('player_friends')
        .insert({ owner_id: session.user.id, friend_id: found.user_id });
      if (e) {
        fail(e.code === '23505' ? 'Tohto hráča už máš medzi priateľmi.' : playerError(e));
        return;
      }
      await usePlayers.getState().refresh();
      if (usePlayers.getState().error) {
        setError(
          'Priateľ je pridaný, ale zoznam sa nepodarilo načítať. Otvor Priateľov a skús načítanie znova.',
        );
        return;
      }
      feedback.success();
      router.dismissTo('/friends');
    });
  const alreadyAdded = !!found && friends.some((f) => f.id === found.user_id);
  return (
    <Page form fallbackHref="/friends" fallbackLabel="Späť k priateľom">
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Pridať priateľa' }} />
      {!ready || loading ? (
        <LoadingRows />
      ) : remoteError ? (
        <View className="gap-4 py-6">
          <Txt className="text-danger" accessibilityRole="alert">
            {remoteError}
          </Txt>
          <Button variant="outline" onPress={() => void refresh()}>
            <Button.Label>Skúsiť znova</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        </View>
      ) : !profile ? (
        <Empty
          text={session ? 'Najprv si vyber svoj nick.' : 'Prihlás sa a nájdi hráčov podľa nicku.'}
          action={session ? 'Vybrať nick' : 'Prihlásiť sa'}
          onPress={() => router.push('/account')}
        />
      ) : (
        <View className="mt-4 gap-5">
          <UserSearch size={36} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
          <TextField isRequired>
            <Label>Nick hráča</Label>
            <Input
              value={nick}
              onChangeText={(v) => {
                setNick(v);
                setFound(null);
                setSearched(false);
                setError('');
              }}
              editable={!pending}
              placeholder="napr. martin_180"
              autoCapitalize="none"
              autoCorrect={false}
              maxLength={25}
              onSubmitEditing={() => {
                if (!pending) void search();
              }}
            />
          </TextField>
          <Button isDisabled={pending} onPress={search}>
            <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Čakaj…' : 'Nájsť hráča'}</Button.Label>
          </Button>
          {!!error && (
            <Txt className="text-danger" accessibilityRole="alert">
              {error}
            </Txt>
          )}
          {found && (
            <View className="border-border gap-4 border-t pt-6">
              <Heading>@{found.nickname}</Heading>
              {alreadyAdded ? (
                <Txt className="text-muted">Tohto hráča už máš medzi priateľmi.</Txt>
              ) : (
                <Button isDisabled={pending} onPress={add}>
                  <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Pridávam…' : 'Pridať priateľa'}</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              )}
            </View>
          )}
          {searched && !found && (
            <Empty
              text="Hráča s týmto nickom sme nenašli."
              action="Vymazať hľadanie"
              onPress={() => {
                setNick('');
                setSearched(false);
              }}
            />
          )}
          <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px]">
            Priateľ musí mať v tejto aplikácii účet a nastavený nick. Pridanie ho uloží do tvojho
            zoznamu, nie automaticky do jeho.
          </Txt>
        </View>
      )}
    </Page>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/match.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, PressableFeedback } from 'heroui-native';
import { Video, X, Delete, Undo2, Check, Trophy } from 'lucide-react-native';
import Animated, { FadeIn, FadeOut } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { Txt, Heading, Empty, StorageNotice, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { CheckoutDarts } from '@/components/CheckoutDarts';
import {
  palette,
  feedback,
  useDarts,
  scores,
  activePlayer,
  average,
  validScore,
  canFinish,
  validCheckout,
} from '@/lib/darts';

export default function Match() {
  const game = useDarts((s) => s.game);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const submitTurn = useDarts((s) => s.submitTurn);
  const undo = useDarts((s) => s.undo);
  const archiveGame = useDarts((s) => s.archiveGame);
  const discardGame = useDarts((s) => s.discardGame);
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [double, setDouble] = useState(false);
  const [darts, setDarts] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(3);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [video, setVideo] = useState(false);
  const [discard, setDiscard] = useState(false);
  if (!hydrated)
    return (
      <View className="bg-background flex-1 p-5">
        <LoadingRows />
      </View>
    );
  if (!game)
    return (
      <View className="bg-background flex-1 p-5">
        <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Zápas' }} />
        <Empty
          text="Terč čaká na prvý zápas."
          action="Nový zápas"
          onPress={() => router.replace('/new-match')}
        />
        <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => router.replace('/')}>
          <Button.Label>Späť na hru</Button.Label>
        </Button>
      </View>
    );
  const currentScores = scores(game);
  const player = activePlayer(game);
  const opponentFirst = game.opponentName.split(' ')[0];
  const practice = game.opponentId === 'practice';
  const name = player === 0 ? 'Ty' : practice ? 'Hráč 2' : opponentFirst;
  const score = Number(input);
  const finishAttempt =
    !!input && score === currentScores[player] && game.doubleOut && canFinish(score, true);
  const checkout =
    !!input &&
    score === currentScores[player] &&
    validScore(score) &&
    (!game.doubleOut || (finishAttempt && double));
  const finished = game.winner !== null;
  const append = (digit: string) => {
    if (input.length >= 3) return;
    setInput(input === '0' ? digit : input + digit);
    setError('');
    setDouble(false);
    setDarts(3);
    feedback.selection();
  };
  const save = () => {
    if (!input || !validScore(score)) {
      setError('Zadaj možné skóre troch šípok od 0 do 180.');
      feedback.error();
      return;
    }
    if (checkout && !validCheckout(score, darts, game.doubleOut)) {
      setError('Toto skóre nemožno zatvoriť zvoleným počtom šípok. Skontroluj výber.');
      feedback.error();
      return;
    }
    submitTurn(score, double, checkout ? darts : 3);
    setInput('');
    setDouble(false);
    setDarts(3);
    setError('');
    if (useDarts.getState().game?.turns.at(-1)?.bust) feedback.error();
    else feedback.success();
  };
  const lastTurn = game.turns.at(-1);
  return (
    <View className="bg-background flex-1">
      <Stack.Screen
        options={{
          title: 'Zápas',
          headerRight: () => (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              isIconOnly
              size="sm"
              accessibilityLabel="Videohovor"
              onPress={() => setVideo((v) => !v)}
            >
              <Video size={22} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
            </Button>
          ),
        }}
      />
      <FlatList
        data={game.turns.toReversed()}
        keyExtractor={(_, index) => `${game.id}-${game.turns.length - index}`}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          paddingBottom: 32,
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
        }}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <StorageNotice />
            <View className="mb-4 flex-row items-center justify-between">
              <Txt style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>
                {game.starting} · {game.doubleOut ? 'Double out' : 'Straight out'}
              </Txt>
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[12px]">Lokálna hra</Txt>
            </View>
            {video && (
              <View className="bg-default mb-4 rounded-2xl p-5">
                <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between">
                  <Video size={26} color={palette.ink} />
                  <Button
                    variant="ghost"
                    size="sm"
                    isIconOnly
                    accessibilityLabel="Zavrieť informácie o videu"
                    onPress={() => setVideo(false)}
                  >
                    <X size={20} color={palette.ink} />
                  </Button>
                </View>
                <Txt style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }} className="mt-3">
                  Videohovor v online zápase
                </Txt>
                <Txt className="text-muted mt-2 text-[13px] leading-5">
                  Pre videohovor pozvi priateľa do online zápasu. Lokálna hra je určená na hranie
                  pri jednom terči.
                </Txt>
                <Button className="mt-3" variant="secondary" onPress={() => router.push('/online')}>
                  <Button.Label>Online zápasy</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            )}
            <View className="bg-ink overflow-hidden rounded-[22px]">
              <View className="flex-row items-center justify-center gap-2 pt-4">
                <View className="bg-accent h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full" />
                <Txt className="text-on-ink text-[12px]">
                  {finished ? 'Zápas dohraný' : `Kolo ${Math.floor(game.turns.length / 2) + 1}`}
                </Txt>
              </View>
              <View className="flex-row px-4 py-4">
                {([0, 1] as const).map((p) => (
                  <View
                    key={p}
                    className={`flex-1 items-center px-2 ${p === 0 ? 'border-on-ink/20 border-r' : ''}`}
                  >
                    <Txt className="text-on-ink text-[14px]" numberOfLines={1}>
                      {p === 0 ? 'Ty' : practice ? 'Hráč 2' : opponentFirst}
                    </Txt>
                    <Txt
                      className={`text-[54px] leading-[66px] ${!finished && player === p ? 'text-accent' : 'text-white'}`}
                      numberOfLines={1}
                      adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      minimumFontScale={0.8}
                      style={{
                        fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold',
                        fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'],
                        letterSpacing: -3,
                      }}
                      maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.15}
                    >
                      {currentScores[p]}
                    </Txt>
                    <Txt className="text-on-ink text-[12px]">Priemer {average(game, p)}</Txt>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>
              <View className="bg-accent px-4 py-3">
                <Txt
                  className="text-center text-[13px]"
                  style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                >
                  {finished
                    ? game.winner === 0
                      ? 'Vyhral si!'
                      : `${practice ? 'Hráč 2' : opponentFirst} vyhráva!`
                    : `Na ťahu: ${name}`}
                </Txt>
              </View>
            </View>
            {finished ? (
              <View className="items-center gap-4 py-6">
                <Trophy size={36} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                <Heading>{game.winner === 0 ? 'Dobrý zásah.' : 'Čas na odvetu.'}</Heading>
                <Button
                  size="lg"
                  className="w-full"
                  onPress={() => {
                    archiveGame();
                    feedback.success();
                    router.dismissTo('/');
                  }}
                >
                  <Button.Label>Uložiť výsledok</Button.Label>
                </Button>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  onPress={() => {
                    undo();
                    setInput('');
                    feedback.success();
                  }}
                >
                  <Undo2 size={18} color={palette.ink} />
                  <Button.Label>Opraviť posledný ťah</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View>
                {lastTurn?.bust && (
                  <Txt className="text-danger mt-4 text-[13px]">
                    Prehodené alebo neplatné ukončenie. Skóre zostáva.
                  </Txt>
                )}
                <View className="mt-5 mb-3 flex-row items-center justify-between">
                  <Txt style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>Súčet 3 šípok</Txt>
                  <Txt
                    className="text-[30px] leading-9"
                    style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold', fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'] }}
                    accessibilityLiveRegion="polite"
                  >
                    {input || '—'}
                  </Txt>
                </View>
                <View className="mb-3 flex-row gap-2">
                  {[26, 41, 60, 100].map((n) => (
                    <Button
                      key={n}
                      variant="secondary"
                      size="sm"
                      className="flex-1 rounded-lg"
                      onPress={() => {
                        setInput(String(n));
                        setDouble(false);
                        setDarts(3);
                        setError('');
                        feedback.selection();
                      }}
                    >
                      <Button.Label>{n}</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                  ))}
                </View>
                {[
                  [1, 2, 3],
                  [4, 5, 6],
                  [7, 8, 9],
                ].map((row) => (
                  <View key={row.join('-')} className="mb-2 flex-row gap-2">
                    {row.map((n) => (
                      <Button
                        key={n}
                        variant="secondary"
                        className="bg-surface h-12 flex-1 rounded-xl"
                        onPress={() => append(String(n))}
                      >
                        <Button.Label
                          className="text-[21px]"
                          style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                        >
                          {n}
                        </Button.Label>
                      </Button>
                    ))}
                  </View>
                ))}
                <View className="mb-3 flex-row gap-2">
                  <Button
                    className="h-12 flex-1 rounded-xl"
                    variant="secondary"
                    isDisabled={!game.turns.length}
                    accessibilityLabel="Vrátiť posledný ťah"
                    onPress={() => {
                      undo();
                      setInput('');
                      setDouble(false);
                      setDarts(3);
                      setError('');
                      feedback.success();
                    }}
                  >
                    <Undo2 size={21} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    className="bg-surface h-12 flex-1 rounded-xl"
                    variant="secondary"
                    onPress={() => append('0')}
                  >
                    <Button.Label
                      className="text-[21px]"
                      style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                    >
                      0
                    </Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                  <Button
                    className="h-12 flex-1 rounded-xl"
                    variant="secondary"
                    isDisabled={!input}
                    accessibilityLabel="Vymazať poslednú číslicu"
                    onPress={() => {
                      setInput(input.slice(0, -1));
                      setDouble(false);
                      setDarts(3);
                      setError('');
                      feedback.selection();
                    }}
                  >
                    <Delete size={21} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                  </Button>
                </View>
                {finishAttempt && (
                  <PressableFeedback
                    className="flex-row items-center gap-3 py-4"
                    accessibilityRole="checkbox"
                    accessibilityState={{ checked: double }}
                    onPress={() => {
                      setDouble((v) => !v);
                      setDarts(3);
                      feedback.selection();
                    }}
                  >
                    <View
                      className={`size-6 items-center justify-center rounded-md border ${double ? 'bg-accent border-accent' : 'border-muted'}`}
                    >
                      {double && <Check size={18} color={palette.ink} />}
                    </View>
                    <Txt className="flex-1 text-[14px]">Posledná šípka trafila double</Txt>
                  </PressableFeedback>
                )}
                {checkout && (
                  <View className="mb-4">
                    <CheckoutDarts
                      value={darts}
                      onChange={(n) => {
                        setDarts(n);
                        setError('');
                      }}
                    />
                  </View>
                )}
                {!!error && (
                  <Txt className="text-danger mb-3 text-[13px]" accessibilityRole="alert">
                    {error}
                  </Txt>
                )}
                <Button size="lg" className="rounded-xl" isDisabled={!input} onPress={save}>
                  <Button.Label>{input ? `Zapísať ${input}` : 'Zapísať skóre'}</Button.Label>
                  <Check size={21} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                </Button>
                {!game.turns.length && (
                  <Txt className="text-muted mt-3 text-center text-[12px]">
                    Žiadny zásah? Zapíš 0.
                  </Txt>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
            {game.turns.length > 0 && (
              <View className="mt-7 mb-2">
                <Heading className="text-[20px]">Priebeh zápasu</Heading>
                <View className="mt-3 flex-row justify-between">
                  <Txt className="text-muted text-[12px]">Hráč / skóre ťahu</Txt>
                  <Txt className="text-muted text-[12px]">Zostáva</Txt>
                </View>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <Animated.View
            entering={FadeIn.duration(160)}
            exiting={FadeOut.duration(100)}
            className="border-border flex-row items-center gap-3 border-b py-3"
          >
            <Txt className="flex-1 text-[14px]" numberOfLines={1}>
              {item.player === 0 ? 'Ty' : practice ? 'Hráč 2' : opponentFirst}
            </Txt>
            <Txt className={item.bust ? 'text-danger text-[14px]' : 'text-[14px]'}>
              {item.bust ? `Prehodené (${item.score})` : item.score}
            </Txt>
            <Txt
              className="w-12 text-right text-[14px]"
              style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
            >
              {item.remaining}
            </Txt>
          </Animated.View>
        )}
        ListFooterComponent={
          <View className="mt-6 gap-2">
            {discard ? (
              <View className="gap-3">
                <Txt>Zahodiť tento zápas bez výsledku?</Txt>
                <Button
                  variant="danger"
                  onPress={() => {
                    discardGame();
                    feedback.success();
                    router.dismissTo('/');
                  }}
                >
                  <Button.Label>Zahodiť zápas</Button.Label>
                </Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => setDiscard(false)}>
                  <Button.Label>Pokračovať</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => router.dismissTo('/')}>
                  <Button.Label>Späť na hru</Button.Label>
                </Button>
                {!finished && (
                  <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => setDiscard(true)}>
                    <Button.Label className="text-muted">Zahodiť zápas</Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </View>
        }
      />
    </View>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/new-match.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { View, FlatList } from 'react-native';
import { Stack, router, useLocalSearchParams } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, PressableFeedback } from 'heroui-native';
import { Check, Target, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Page, Heading, Txt, Avatar, LoadingRows, StorageNotice } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { feedback, palette, useDarts, type Friend } from '@/lib/darts';
import { usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';

const practice: Friend = {
  id: 'practice',
  name: 'Tréningový hráč',
  handle: 'trening',
  color: 'blue',
  demo: false,
};
export default function NewMatch() {
  const { friendId } = useLocalSearchParams<{ friendId?: string }>();
  const localFriends = useDarts((s) => s.friends);
  const remoteFriends = usePlayers((s) => s.friends);
  const friends = [...remoteFriends, ...localFriends];
  const game = useDarts((s) => s.game);
  const hydrated = useDarts((s) => s.hydrated);
  const startGame = useDarts((s) => s.startGame);
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(friendId ?? friends[0]?.id ?? 'practice');
  const [starting, setStarting] = useState<301 | 501>(501);
  const [doubleOut, setDoubleOut] = useState(true);
  const [replace, setReplace] = useState(false);
  const opponent = friends.find((f) => f.id === selected) ?? practice;
  if (!hydrated)
    return (
      <Page>
        <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Nový zápas' }} />
        <LoadingRows />
      </Page>
    );
  return (
    <View className="bg-background flex-1">
      <Stack.Screen options={{ title: 'Nový zápas' }} />
      <FlatList
        data={[...friends, practice]}
        keyExtractor={(friend) => friend.id}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          paddingBottom: Math.max(insets.bottom, 32),
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
        }}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <StorageNotice />
            <Heading className="mt-2 mb-4">S kým hráš?</Heading>
            <Txt className="text-muted mb-4 text-[13px]">
              Lokálny zápas · skóre zadávaš za oboch hráčov
            </Txt>
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item: friend }) => (
          <PressableFeedback
            onPress={() => {
              if (selected !== friend.id) {
                setSelected(friend.id);
                feedback.selection();
              }
            }}
            className={`mb-2 flex-row items-center gap-3 rounded-xl p-3 ${selected === friend.id ? 'bg-surface' : ''}`}
            accessibilityRole="radio"
            accessibilityState={{ selected: selected === friend.id }}
          >
            <Avatar friend={friend} size={42} />
            <View className="flex-1">
              <Txt style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }} numberOfLines={1}>
                {friend.id === 'practice' ? 'Dvaja pri jednom terči' : friend.name}
              </Txt>
              {friend.demo && <Txt className="text-muted mt-1 text-[12px]">Ukážkový hráč</Txt>}
            </View>
            {selected === friend.id && <Check size={22} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />}
          </PressableFeedback>
        )}
        ListFooterComponent={
          <View>
            <Heading className="mt-7 mb-4 text-[20px]">Hra</Heading>
            <View className="flex-row gap-3">
              {([301, 501] as const).map((n) => (
                <Button
                  key={n}
                  className="h-16 flex-1 rounded-xl"
                  variant={starting === n ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                  onPress={() => {
                    if (starting !== n) {
                      setStarting(n);
                      feedback.selection();
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  <Button.Label className="text-[24px]" style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold' }}>
                    {n}
                  </Button.Label>
                </Button>
              ))}
            </View>
            <Heading className="mt-7 mb-4 text-[20px]">Ukončenie hry</Heading>
            <View className="flex-row gap-3">
              {[true, false].map((d) => (
                <Button
                  key={String(d)}
                  className="flex-1 rounded-xl"
                  variant={doubleOut === d ? 'primary' : 'secondary'}
                  onPress={() => {
                    if (doubleOut !== d) {
                      setDoubleOut(d);
                      feedback.selection();
                    }
                  }}
                >
                  <Button.Label numberOfLines={1}>{d ? 'Double out' : 'Straight out'}</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              ))}
            </View>
            <Txt className="text-muted mt-3 mb-7 text-[13px]">
              {doubleOut
                ? 'Posledná šípka musí trafiť double alebo bull za 50.'
                : 'Na výhru stačí znížiť skóre presne na nulu.'}
            </Txt>
            {game && !replace ? (
              <View className="gap-3">
                <Txt>
                  {game.winner !== null
                    ? 'Máš dohraný zápas, ktorý čaká na uloženie.'
                    : 'Máš rozhraný zápas.'}
                </Txt>
                <Button onPress={() => router.push('/match')}>
                  <Button.Label>
                    {game.winner !== null ? 'Zobraziť výsledok' : 'Pokračovať v zápase'}
                  </Button.Label>
                  <ChevronRight size={20} color={palette.ink} />
                </Button>
                <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => setReplace(true)}>
                  <Button.Label>Začať odznova</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <View className="gap-3">
                {replace && (
                  <Txt className="text-danger text-[13px]">
                    Začatím nového zápasu zahodíš predchádzajúci zápas.
                  </Txt>
                )}
                <Button
                  size="lg"
                  onPress={() => {
                    startGame(opponent, starting, doubleOut);
                    feedback.success();
                    router.replace('/match');
                  }}
                >
                  <Target size={20} color={palette.ink} strokeWidth={1.8} />
                  <Button.Label>Začať zápas</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            )}
            <Button className="mt-5" variant="ghost" onPress={() => router.dismissTo('/')}>
              <Button.Label>Späť na hru</Button.Label>
            </Button>
          </View>
        }
      />
    </View>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: app/online/[id].tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { AppState, View, FlatList, KeyboardAvoidingView, Platform } from 'react-native';
import { router, useLocalSearchParams, useFocusEffect } from 'expo-router';
import { Button, Input, Label, Switch } from 'heroui-native';
import { Video, Trophy } from 'lucide-react-native';
import { Page, Heading, Txt, Empty, LoadingRows } from '@/components/DartsUI';
import { OnlineGate, OnlineError, Presence, SyncStatus } from '@/components/OnlineUI';
import VideoCall from '@/components/VideoCall';
import { CheckoutDarts } from '@/components/CheckoutDarts';
import { bilt } from '@/lib/bilt';
import { feedback, palette, validScore, canFinish } from '@/lib/darts';
import { playerError, usePlayers } from '@/lib/players';
import {
  appIsActive,
  loadMatch,
  opponentId,
  opponentName,
  respondMatch,
  useOnlineQuery,
} from '@/lib/online';

export default function OnlineGame() {
  const { id = '' } = useLocalSearchParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const userId = usePlayers((s) => s.session?.user.id);
  const query = useOnlineQuery(
    userId ? `match:${userId}:${id}` : null,
    useCallback(() => loadMatch(id), [id]),
  );
  const [input, setInput] = useState('');
  const [double, setDouble] = useState(false);
  const [darts, setDarts] = useState<1 | 2 | 3>(3);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [video, setVideo] = useState(false);
  const [abandon, setAbandon] = useState(false);
  const [foreground, setForeground] = useState(appIsActive());
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState(false);
  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      setFocused(true);
      return () => setFocused(false);
    }, []),
  );
  const m = query.data?.match;
  const [previousTurn, setPreviousTurn] = useState({ turnCount: m?.turn_count, id, userId });
  if (
    previousTurn.turnCount !== m?.turn_count ||
    previousTurn.id !== id ||
    previousTurn.userId !== userId
  ) {
    setPreviousTurn({ turnCount: m?.turn_count, id, userId });
    setInput('');
    setDouble(false);
    setDarts(3);
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    const change = () => setForeground(appIsActive());
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener('change', change);
    if (Platform.OS === 'web') document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', change);
    return () => {
      sub.remove();
      if (Platform.OS === 'web') document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', change);
    };
  }, []);
  const respond = async (decision: 'accept' | 'decline' | 'cancel' | 'abandon') => {
    if (pending) return;
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      await respondMatch(id, decision);
      await query.refresh();
      feedback.success();
      setAbandon(false);
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        playerError({
          message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Zápas sa nepodarilo aktualizovať.',
        }),
      );
      feedback.error();
      await query.refresh();
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  const submit = async () => {
    if (!m || pending) return;
    const score = Number(input);
    if (!input || !/^\d{1,3}$/.test(input) || !validScore(score)) {
      setError('Zadaj možné skóre od 0 do 180.');
      feedback.error();
      return;
    }
    setPending(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const { data, error: e } = await bilt.rpc('submit_online_turn', {
        mid: id,
        expected_turn: m.turn_count,
        turn_score: score,
        finished_double: double,
        darts_thrown: darts,
      });
      if (e) throw new Error(e.message);
      if (!data?.[0])
        throw new Error('Ťah sa nezapísal. Skontroluj skóre, počet šípok a kto je na ťahu.');
      await query.refresh();
      setInput('');
      setDouble(false);
      setDarts(3);
      feedback.success();
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        playerError({ message: e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Skóre sa nepodarilo odoslať.' }),
      );
      feedback.error();
      await query.refresh();
    } finally {
      setPending(false);
    }
  };
  if (!userId)
    return (
      <Page fallbackHref="/online" fallbackLabel="Späť na online zápasy">
        <OnlineGate>{null}</OnlineGate>
      </Page>
    );
  if (query.loading)
    return (
      <Page fallbackHref="/online">
        <LoadingRows />
      </Page>
    );
  if (!m)
    return (
      <Page fallbackHref="/online" fallbackLabel="Späť na online zápasy">
        <OnlineError error={query.error} retry={() => void query.refresh()} />
        {!query.error && (
          <Empty
            text="Zápas nie je dostupný pre tento účet."
            action="Online zápasy"
            onPress={() => router.replace('/online')}
          />
        )}
      </Page>
    );
  const host = m.host_id === userId;
  const myRemaining = host ? m.host_remaining : m.guest_remaining;
  const peerRemaining = host ? m.guest_remaining : m.host_remaining;
  const peerName = opponentName(m, userId);
  const contact = query.data?.contacts.find((c) => c.user_id === opponentId(m, userId));
  const mine = m.next_player === userId && m.status === 'active';
  const checkout =
    !!input &&
    Number(input) === myRemaining &&
    canFinish(Number(input), m.double_out) &&
    (!m.double_out || double);
  const showDouble =
    !!input && Number(input) === myRemaining && m.double_out && canFinish(Number(input), true);
  const disabled = pending || !!query.error || !mine;
  return (
    <KeyboardAvoidingView
      className="bg-background flex-1"
      behavior={Platform.OS === 'ios' ? 'padding' : undefined}
      keyboardVerticalOffset={90}
    >
      <FlatList
        data={query.data?.turns ?? []}
        keyExtractor={(t) => String(t.turn_number)}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          padding: 20,
          paddingBottom: 32,
          maxWidth: 600,
          width: '100%',
          alignSelf: 'center',
        }}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
              <Txt className="flex-1" style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}>
                {m.starting} · {m.double_out ? 'Double out' : 'Straight out'}
              </Txt>
              <Presence online={!!contact?.online} unknown={!!query.error || !contact} />
            </View>
            <SyncStatus error={query.error} syncedAt={query.syncedAt} />
            <OnlineError error={query.error} retry={() => void query.refresh()} />
            <View className="bg-ink overflow-hidden rounded-[22px]">
              <View className="flex-row p-4">
                {[
                  { name: 'Ty', score: myRemaining, active: mine },
                  {
                    name: `@${peerName}`,
                    score: peerRemaining,
                    active: !mine && m.status === 'active',
                  },
                ].map((p) => (
                  <View key={p.name} className="flex-1 items-center px-2">
                    <Txt className="text-on-ink text-[14px]" numberOfLines={1}>
                      {p.name}
                    </Txt>
                    <Txt
                      className={
                        p.active
                          ? 'text-accent text-[54px] leading-[66px]'
                          : 'text-[54px] leading-[66px] text-white'
                      }
                      adjustsFontSizeToFit
                      numberOfLines={1}
                      maxFontSizeMultiplier={1.15}
                      style={{
                        fontFamily: 'Inter_700Bold',
                        fontVariant: ['tabular-nums'],
                        letterSpacing: -3,
                      }}
                    >
                      {p.score}
                    </Txt>
                  </View>
                ))}
              </View>
              <View className="bg-accent px-4 py-3">
                <Txt
                  className="text-center text-[13px]"
                  style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
                >
                  {m.status === 'invited'
                    ? 'Čaká na prijatie'
                    : m.status === 'finished'
                      ? m.winner_id === userId
                        ? 'Vyhral si!'
                        : `@${peerName} vyhráva!`
                      : m.status === 'active'
                        ? mine
                          ? 'Si na ťahu'
                          : `Na ťahu: @${peerName}`
                        : m.status === 'declined'
                          ? 'Pozvánka odmietnutá'
                          : 'Zápas zrušený'}
                </Txt>
              </View>
            </View>
            {m.status === 'invited' && (
              <View className="mt-5 gap-3">
                <Heading className="text-[20px]">
                  {host ? 'Pozvánka odoslaná' : `@${peerName} ťa pozýva`}
                </Heading>
                {host ? (
                  <Button
                    variant="secondary"
                    isDisabled={pending || !!query.error}
                    onPress={() => void respond('cancel')}
                  >
                    <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Čakaj…' : 'Zrušiť pozvánku'}</Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Button
                      isDisabled={pending || !!query.error}
                      onPress={() => void respond('accept')}
                    >
                      <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Prijímam…' : 'Prijať zápas'}</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      variant="ghost"
                      isDisabled={pending || !!query.error}
                      onPress={() => void respond('decline')}
                    >
                      <Button.Label>Teraz nie</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                  </>
                )}
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  onPress={() =>
                    router.push({
                      pathname: '/conversation/[id]',
                      params: { id: opponentId(m, userId) },
                    })
                  }
                >
                  <Button.Label>Napísať súperovi</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            )}
            {m.status === 'active' && (
              <View className="mt-5 gap-4">
                <Button
                  variant="secondary"
                  onPress={() => {
                    setVideo((v) => !v);
                    feedback.selection();
                  }}
                >
                  <Video size={19} strokeWidth={1.8} color={palette.ink} />
                  <Button.Label>
                    {video ? 'Odpojiť videohovor' : 'Pripojiť videohovor'}
                  </Button.Label>
                </Button>
                {video && focused && foreground && (
                  <VideoCall matchId={id} onLeave={() => setVideo(false)} />
                )}
                <Txt className="text-muted text-[13px]">
                  {mine
                    ? 'Zapisuješ iba svoje hody. Odoslaný ťah už nemožno meniť.'
                    : 'Čakáš na súperov ťah. Hra zostane uložená aj po odchode.'}
                </Txt>
                {mine && (
                  <>
                    <View>
                      <Label>Súčet 3 šípok</Label>
                      <Input
                        value={input}
                        keyboardType="number-pad"
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        maxLength={3}
                        editable={!disabled}
                        onChangeText={(v) => {
                          setInput(v.replace(/\D/g, ''));
                          setDouble(false);
                          setDarts(3);
                          setError('');
                        }}
                        placeholder="0 až 180"
                        accessibilityLabel="Skóre ťahu"
                        className="mt-2"
                      />
                    </View>
                    <View className="flex-row gap-2">
                      {[26, 60, 100, 180].map((n) => (
                        <Button
                          key={n}
                          variant="secondary"
                          className="flex-1"
                          size="sm"
                          isDisabled={disabled}
                          onPress={() => {
                            setInput(String(n));
                            setDouble(false);
                            setDarts(3);
                            feedback.selection();
                          }}
                        >
                          <Button.Label>{n}</Button.Label>
                        </Button>
                      ))}
                    </View>
                    {(showDouble || checkout) && (
                      <View className="gap-4">
                        {m.double_out && (
                          <View className="flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3">
                            <Txt className="flex-1">Posledná šípka bola double</Txt>
                            <Switch
                              isSelected={double}
                              isDisabled={disabled}
                              onSelectedChange={(v) => {
                                setDouble(v);
                                setDarts(3);
                                feedback.selection();
                              }}
                            />
                          </View>
                        )}
                        {checkout && (
                          <CheckoutDarts value={darts} onChange={setDarts} disabled={disabled} />
                        )}
                      </View>
                    )}
                    <Button isDisabled={disabled || !input} onPress={() => void submit()}>
                      <Button.Label>{pending ? 'Odosielam…' : 'Potvrdiť skóre'}</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                  </>
                )}
                {abandon ? (
                  <View className="gap-3">
                    <Txt>Zrušiť zápas pre oboch hráčov? Nebude sa počítať do štatistík.</Txt>
                    <Button
                      variant="danger"
                      isDisabled={pending || !!query.error}
                      onPress={() => void respond('abandon')}
                    >
                      <Button.Label>Zrušiť zápas</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                    <Button variant="ghost" isDisabled={pending} onPress={() => setAbandon(false)}>
                      <Button.Label>Pokračovať</Button.Label>
                    </Button>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <Button variant="ghost" onPress={() => setAbandon(true)}>
                    <Button.Label>Zrušiť rozhraný zápas</Button.Label>
                  </Button>
                )}
              </View>
            )}
            {m.status === 'finished' && (
              <View className="items-center gap-3 py-6">
                <Trophy size={32} strokeWidth={1.8} color={palette.ink} />
                <Txt>Výsledok a štatistiky sú uložené.</Txt>
                <Button onPress={() => router.push('/statistics')}>
                  <Button.Label>Moje štatistiky</Button.Label>
                </Button>
              </View>
            )}
            {!!error && (
              <Txt className="text-danger mt-4" accessibilityRole="alert">
                {error}
              </Txt>
            )}
            <Heading className="mt-7 mb-3 text-[20px]">Ťahy</Heading>
          </View>
        }
        renderItem={({ item }) => (
          <View className="border-border flex-row justify-between gap-3 border-b py-3">
            <View className="flex-1">
              <Txt numberOfLines={1}>{item.player_id === userId ? 'Ty' : `@${peerName}`}</Txt>
              <Txt className="text-muted text-[12px]">
                Ťah {item.turn_number} · {item.darts} {item.darts === 1 ? 'šípka' : 'šípky'}
              </Txt>
            </View>
            <Txt
              style={{ fontFamily: 'Inter_600SemiBold' }}
              className={item.bust ? 'text-danger' : ''}
            >
              {item.bust ? 'Prehodené' : item.score} → {item.remaining}
            </Txt>
          </View>
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={<Txt className="text-muted py-4 text-[13px]">Zatiaľ bez hodov.</Txt>}
        ListFooterComponent={
          <Button variant="ghost" className="mt-4" onPress={() => router.dismissTo('/online')}>
            <Button.Label>Späť na online zápasy</Button.Label>
          </Button>
        }
      />
    </KeyboardAvoidingView>
  );
}
