-- Oche public database source snapshot, retrieved 2026-11-02.
-- Reference definitions, NOT an ordered installation migration.
-- Table dependencies and SQL-function dependencies require migration ordering.
-- No user records, secrets or managed auth/storage implementation included.

CREATE TABLE public.direct_messages (
  id bigint GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY NOT NULL,
  request_id uuid NOT NULL,
  sender_id uuid NOT NULL,
  recipient_id uuid NOT NULL,
  body text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_body_check CHECK (((length(TRIM(BOTH FROM body)) >= 1) AND (length(TRIM(BOTH FROM body)) <= 2000)));
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_check CHECK ((sender_id <> recipient_id));
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id);
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_recipient_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (recipient_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_request_id_key UNIQUE (request_id);
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ADD CONSTRAINT direct_messages_sender_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (sender_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
CREATE INDEX direct_messages_pair_idx ON public.direct_messages USING btree (sender_id, recipient_id, id DESC);
CREATE INDEX direct_messages_recipient_idx ON public.direct_messages USING btree (recipient_id, id DESC);
ALTER TABLE public.direct_messages ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY message_participants ON public.direct_messages AS PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (((sender_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)) OR (recipient_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid))));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.direct_messages TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.direct_messages TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.direct_messages TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.direct_messages TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE public.online_matches (
  id uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
  host_id uuid NOT NULL,
  guest_id uuid NOT NULL,
  host_nickname text NOT NULL,
  guest_nickname text NOT NULL,
  starting integer NOT NULL,
  double_out boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL,
  status text DEFAULT 'invited'::text NOT NULL,
  host_remaining integer NOT NULL,
  guest_remaining integer NOT NULL,
  next_player uuid NOT NULL,
  winner_id uuid,
  turn_count integer DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
  updated_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
  completed_at timestamp with time zone
);
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_check CHECK ((host_id <> guest_id));
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_check1 CHECK (((host_remaining >= 0) AND (guest_remaining >= 0)));
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_guest_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (guest_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_host_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (host_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_next_player_fkey FOREIGN KEY (next_player) REFERENCES auth.users(id);
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_pkey PRIMARY KEY (id);
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_starting_check CHECK ((starting = ANY (ARRAY[301, 501])));
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_status_check CHECK ((status = ANY (ARRAY['invited'::text, 'active'::text, 'finished'::text, 'declined'::text, 'cancelled'::text])));
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ADD CONSTRAINT online_matches_winner_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (winner_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id);
CREATE INDEX online_matches_host_idx ON public.online_matches USING btree (host_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE INDEX online_matches_guest_idx ON public.online_matches USING btree (guest_id, created_at DESC);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX online_match_pair_open ON public.online_matches USING btree (LEAST(host_id, guest_id), GREATEST(host_id, guest_id)) WHERE (status = ANY (ARRAY['invited'::text, 'active'::text]));
ALTER TABLE public.online_matches ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY match_participants ON public.online_matches AS PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (((host_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)) OR (guest_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid))));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.online_matches TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.online_matches TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.online_matches TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.online_matches TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE public.online_turns (
  match_id uuid NOT NULL,
  turn_number integer NOT NULL,
  player_id uuid NOT NULL,
  score integer NOT NULL,
  darts integer NOT NULL,
  bust boolean NOT NULL,
  remaining integer NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ADD CONSTRAINT online_turns_darts_check CHECK (((darts >= 1) AND (darts <= 3)));
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ADD CONSTRAINT online_turns_match_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (match_id) REFERENCES online_matches(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ADD CONSTRAINT online_turns_pkey PRIMARY KEY (match_id, turn_number);
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ADD CONSTRAINT online_turns_player_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (player_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id);
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ADD CONSTRAINT online_turns_score_check CHECK (((score >= 0) AND (score <= 180)));
ALTER TABLE public.online_turns ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY turn_participants ON public.online_turns AS PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (is_match_player(match_id));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.online_turns TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.online_turns TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.online_turns TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.online_turns TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE public.player_friends (
  owner_id uuid NOT NULL,
  friend_id uuid NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.player_friends ADD CONSTRAINT player_friends_check CHECK ((owner_id <> friend_id));
ALTER TABLE public.player_friends ADD CONSTRAINT player_friends_friend_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (friend_id) REFERENCES player_profiles(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.player_friends ADD CONSTRAINT player_friends_owner_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (owner_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.player_friends ADD CONSTRAINT player_friends_pkey PRIMARY KEY (owner_id, friend_id);
ALTER TABLE public.player_friends ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY friends_owner ON public.player_friends AS PERMISSIVE FOR ALL TO authenticated USING ((owner_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid))) WITH CHECK ((owner_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.player_friends TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.player_friends TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.player_friends TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.player_friends TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE public.player_presence (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  last_seen timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
  is_active boolean DEFAULT true NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.player_presence ADD CONSTRAINT player_presence_pkey PRIMARY KEY (user_id);
ALTER TABLE public.player_presence ADD CONSTRAINT player_presence_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.player_presence ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY presence_owner ON public.player_presence AS PERMISSIVE FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING ((user_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.player_presence TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.player_presence TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.player_presence TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.player_presence TO authenticated;

CREATE TABLE public.player_profiles (
  user_id uuid NOT NULL,
  nickname text NOT NULL,
  created_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL
);
ALTER TABLE public.player_profiles ADD CONSTRAINT player_profiles_nickname_check CHECK ((nickname ~ '^[a-z0-9_]{3,24}$'::text));
ALTER TABLE public.player_profiles ADD CONSTRAINT player_profiles_nickname_key UNIQUE (nickname);
ALTER TABLE public.player_profiles ADD CONSTRAINT player_profiles_pkey PRIMARY KEY (user_id);
ALTER TABLE public.player_profiles ADD CONSTRAINT player_profiles_user_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE;
ALTER TABLE public.player_profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
CREATE POLICY profile_owner ON public.player_profiles AS PERMISSIVE FOR ALL TO authenticated USING ((user_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid))) WITH CHECK ((user_id = ( SELECT auth.uid() AS uid)));
GRANT DELETE ON TABLE public.player_profiles TO authenticated;
GRANT INSERT ON TABLE public.player_profiles TO authenticated;
GRANT SELECT ON TABLE public.player_profiles TO authenticated;
GRANT UPDATE ON TABLE public.player_profiles TO authenticated;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.can_contact_player(peer uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT auth.role()='authenticated' AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND peer<>auth.uid() AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.player_friends WHERE (owner_id=auth.uid() AND friend_id=peer) OR (owner_id=peer AND friend_id=auth.uid())) $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.find_player_by_nickname(search_nickname text)
 RETURNS TABLE(user_id uuid, nickname text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT p.user_id,p.nickname FROM public.player_profiles p WHERE auth.role() = 'authenticated' AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND p.nickname = lower(trim(search_nickname)) AND p.user_id <> auth.uid() LIMIT 1 $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.invite_online_match(peer uuid, starting_score integer DEFAULT 501, use_double_out boolean DEFAULT true)
 RETURNS SETOF online_matches
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ INSERT INTO public.online_matches(host_id,guest_id,host_nickname,guest_nickname,starting,double_out,host_remaining,guest_remaining,next_player) SELECT auth.uid(),peer,h.nickname,g.nickname,starting_score,use_double_out,starting_score,starting_score,auth.uid() FROM public.player_profiles h CROSS JOIN public.player_profiles g WHERE h.user_id=auth.uid() AND g.user_id=peer AND public.can_contact_player(peer) AND starting_score IN(301,501) AND use_double_out IS NOT NULL ON CONFLICT(least(host_id,guest_id),greatest(host_id,guest_id)) WHERE status IN('invited','active') DO UPDATE SET updated_at=online_matches.updated_at WHERE online_matches.host_id=auth.uid() OR online_matches.guest_id=auth.uid() RETURNING * $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.is_match_player(mid uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT auth.role()='authenticated' AND EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.online_matches WHERE id=mid AND (host_id=auth.uid() OR guest_id=auth.uid())) $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.list_player_friends()
 RETURNS TABLE(user_id uuid, nickname text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT p.user_id,p.nickname FROM public.player_friends f JOIN public.player_profiles p ON p.user_id=f.friend_id WHERE auth.role() = 'authenticated' AND f.owner_id=auth.uid() ORDER BY p.nickname $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.list_social_contacts()
 RETURNS TABLE(user_id uuid, nickname text, online boolean, last_seen timestamp with time zone, last_body text, last_message_at timestamp with time zone, last_sender_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT p.user_id,p.nickname,coalesce(pr.is_active AND pr.last_seen>now()-interval '60 seconds',false),pr.last_seen,l.body,l.created_at,l.sender_id FROM public.player_profiles p LEFT JOIN public.player_presence pr ON pr.user_id=p.user_id LEFT JOIN LATERAL(SELECT m.body,m.created_at,m.sender_id FROM public.direct_messages m WHERE (m.sender_id=auth.uid() AND m.recipient_id=p.user_id) OR (m.recipient_id=auth.uid() AND m.sender_id=p.user_id) ORDER BY m.id DESC LIMIT 1) l ON true WHERE auth.role()='authenticated' AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND p.user_id<>auth.uid() AND (EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.player_friends f WHERE f.owner_id=auth.uid() AND f.friend_id=p.user_id) OR l.created_at IS NOT NULL OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM public.online_matches g WHERE (g.host_id=auth.uid() AND g.guest_id=p.user_id) OR (g.guest_id=auth.uid() AND g.host_id=p.user_id))) ORDER BY l.created_at DESC NULLS LAST,p.nickname $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.my_online_statistics()
 RETURNS TABLE(matches bigint, wins bigint, losses bigint, three_dart_average numeric, highest_score integer, scores_180 bigint)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ WITH games AS(SELECT * FROM public.online_matches WHERE status='finished' AND (host_id=auth.uid() OR guest_id=auth.uid()) AND auth.role()='authenticated'), turns AS(SELECT t.* FROM public.online_turns t JOIN games g ON g.id=t.match_id WHERE t.player_id=auth.uid()) SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM games),(SELECT count(*) FROM games WHERE winner_id=auth.uid()),(SELECT count(*) FROM games WHERE winner_id<>auth.uid()),coalesce(round(3.0*sum(CASE WHEN bust THEN 0 ELSE score END)/nullif(sum(darts),0),1),0),coalesce(max(CASE WHEN bust THEN 0 ELSE score END),0),count(*) FILTER(WHERE score=180 AND NOT bust) FROM turns $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.online_checkout_possible(total integer, dart_count integer, double_finish boolean)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ WITH hits AS(SELECT 0 v UNION SELECT generate_series(1,20) UNION SELECT generate_series(1,20)*2 UNION SELECT generate_series(1,20)*3 UNION SELECT 25 UNION SELECT 50),last_hits AS(SELECT v FROM hits WHERE NOT double_finish UNION SELECT generate_series(1,20)*2 WHERE double_finish UNION SELECT 50 WHERE double_finish) SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM hits a CROSS JOIN hits b CROSS JOIN last_hits c WHERE a.v+b.v+c.v=total AND (dart_count>=3 OR a.v=0) AND (dart_count>=2 OR b.v=0)) $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.online_turn_bust(total integer, previous integer, double_out boolean, finished_double boolean)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ SELECT total>previous OR (double_out AND (previous-total=1 OR(previous=total AND (NOT finished_double OR NOT public.online_checkout_possible(total,3,true))))) $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.respond_online_match(mid uuid, decision text)
 RETURNS SETOF online_matches
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ UPDATE public.online_matches SET status=CASE decision WHEN 'accept' THEN 'active' WHEN 'decline' THEN 'declined' ELSE 'cancelled' END,updated_at=now() WHERE id=mid AND auth.role()='authenticated' AND ((status='invited' AND guest_id=auth.uid() AND decision IN('accept','decline')) OR(status='invited' AND host_id=auth.uid() AND decision='cancel') OR(status='active' AND auth.uid() IN(host_id,guest_id) AND decision='abandon')) RETURNING * $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.send_direct_message(peer uuid, message_body text, client_request uuid)
 RETURNS SETOF direct_messages
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ INSERT INTO public.direct_messages(request_id,sender_id,recipient_id,body) SELECT client_request,auth.uid(),peer,trim(message_body) WHERE public.can_contact_player(peer) AND length(trim(message_body)) BETWEEN 1 AND 2000 AND client_request IS NOT NULL ON CONFLICT(request_id) DO UPDATE SET body=direct_messages.body WHERE direct_messages.sender_id=auth.uid() AND direct_messages.recipient_id=peer AND direct_messages.body=trim(message_body) RETURNING * $function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.submit_online_turn(mid uuid, expected_turn integer, turn_score integer, finished_double boolean DEFAULT false, darts_thrown integer DEFAULT 3)
 RETURNS SETOF online_matches
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$
 WITH locked AS(SELECT * FROM public.online_matches WHERE id=mid AND auth.role()='authenticated' AND next_player=auth.uid() AND status='active' AND turn_count=expected_turn AND auth.uid() IN(host_id,guest_id) FOR UPDATE), input AS(SELECT l.*,CASE WHEN host_id=auth.uid() THEN host_remaining ELSE guest_remaining END previous FROM locked l), checked AS(SELECT i.*,public.online_turn_bust(turn_score,previous,double_out,finished_double) is_bust FROM input i WHERE turn_score BETWEEN 0 AND 180 AND turn_score NOT IN(169,172,173,175,176,178,179) AND darts_thrown BETWEEN 1 AND 3 AND finished_double IS NOT NULL), eligible AS(SELECT c.*,CASE WHEN is_bust THEN previous ELSE previous-turn_score END new_remaining FROM checked c WHERE (darts_thrown=3 OR(previous=turn_score AND NOT is_bust)) AND (previous<>turn_score OR is_bust OR public.online_checkout_possible(turn_score,darts_thrown,double_out))), updated AS(UPDATE public.online_matches m SET host_remaining=CASE WHEN m.host_id=auth.uid() THEN e.new_remaining ELSE m.host_remaining END,guest_remaining=CASE WHEN m.guest_id=auth.uid() THEN e.new_remaining ELSE m.guest_remaining END,turn_count=m.turn_count+1,next_player=CASE WHEN m.host_id=auth.uid() THEN m.guest_id ELSE m.host_id END,status=CASE WHEN e.new_remaining=0 THEN 'finished' ELSE 'active' END,winner_id=CASE WHEN e.new_remaining=0 THEN auth.uid() ELSE NULL END,completed_at=CASE WHEN e.new_remaining=0 THEN now() ELSE NULL END,updated_at=now() FROM eligible e WHERE m.id=e.id AND m.turn_count=expected_turn AND m.next_player=auth.uid() AND m.status='active' RETURNING m AS match,e.is_bust),saved AS(INSERT INTO public.online_turns(match_id,turn_number,player_id,score,darts,bust,remaining) SELECT (u.match).id,(u.match).turn_count,auth.uid(),turn_score,darts_thrown,u.is_bust,CASE WHEN (u.match).host_id=auth.uid() THEN (u.match).host_remaining ELSE (u.match).guest_remaining END FROM updated u RETURNING match_id) SELECT (u.match).* FROM updated u JOIN saved s ON s.match_id=(u.match).id
$function$;

CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.touch_presence(active boolean DEFAULT true)
 RETURNS SETOF player_presence
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public'
AS $function$ INSERT INTO public.player_presence(user_id,last_seen,is_active) SELECT auth.uid(),now(),active WHERE auth.role()='authenticated' AND auth.uid() IS NOT NULL AND active IS NOT NULL ON CONFLICT(user_id) DO UPDATE SET last_seen=now(),is_active=EXCLUDED.is_active RETURNING * $function$;
